|\\n| 拼写质量 | 正常 | 退化（crabs→crbs, skein→skeen）|\\n\\n### 退化模式\\nSparse 模型能生成与问题相关的开头（如 \\\"First, we need to know...\\\"），但随即进入同一句话的无限重复（15+ 次）。Sample 7 中正确算出 9×2=18，但无法继续推理。\\n\\n### 对论文的意义\\n50% sparsity 摧毁了深层推理能力。PPL-downstream disconnect 的机制：PPL 衡量 next-token 平均质量，repetition loop 的每个重复 token 仍有较高概率（模型\\\"自信地\\\"重复），但推理链断裂。\"}, \"diag_gsm8k_gencomp\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", Sparse model suffers FUNDAMENTAL reasoning collapse: (1) 10/10 samples degenerate into repetitive loops within 1-3 sentences; (2) no sample produces correct final answer or completes multi-step reasoning; (3) sparse model can generate topically relevant opening text but immediately enters infinite repetition (e.g. \\\"We can then use the formula...\\\" repeated 15+ times); (4) occasional partial correctness in first sentence (Sample 7: correctly computes 9*2=18 but never adds +3); (5) spelling degradation observed (crabs→crbs, skein→skeen). Dense model generates coherent step-by-step reasoning and often reaches correct answers. |\\n\\n## 重要发现\\nevaluate.py 的 \\\"Dense baseline\\\" 实际是通过 predictor hard mask 的评估（PredictorWrapper._patch_mlps() monkey-patch 所有 MLP，forward_dense() 执行后 sparse_mode 重置为 True）。因此 \\\"dense\\\" PPL neuron。\\n\\n## Per-layer sparsity 分布差异\\n- KL: 非均匀 (0.215-0.806), 中间层低稀疏(保护), 后层高稀疏\\n- BCE: 较均匀 (0.430-0.664), 后层略高\\n\\n## 结论: SUCCESS\\nKL normalized loss 在所有指标上显著优于 BCE: TEAL sparse PPL 12.08 vs
